18:33:57.887 Within a Massive Onyx Tower
18:33:57.887    Walls of solid onyx, polished to a near mirror shine circle the area
18:33:57.888 here, creating an immense tower.  A staircase juts out from the wall,
18:33:57.889 circling it's way upwards around the tower.  At even intervals
18:33:57.890 throughout the climb, braziers of iron have been bolted to the wall.
18:33:57.891 Each brazier gives off wisps of sulfurous smoke as it slowly burns it's
18:33:57.891 fuel.  The faint light from each brazier makes the climb up the stairs
18:33:57.892 treacherous to say the least.  The smooth stone stairs have no railing
18:33:57.893 and in some places are crumbling from age.  The top of the tower is

18:43:48.647 may be easily pushed inside the arcane device. The cube is about six
18:43:48.649 inches in all dimensions, yet items much longer easily fit inside. The
18:43:48.650 aperture through which items may be placed inside the cube is
18:43:48.651 quite pliable and can accommodate even the largest of items.
